U2ICILELO LWESIBOZO.
THE LOVEDALE PRESS.

INTSAYELELO.
Nangani ndingengcali kwathi ni yamthetho, ndinawo 
noko amanakani oku6a umthetho wasemaXhoseni awahluke 
nakancinane kowezizwe ezikhanyiselweyo. Iintlanga 
eziMhlophe zithe zakufika kweli lizwe zafumana uku6a 
a6antu 6eli lizwe 6aphantse uku6a ziincutfhe zomthetho 
6onke, namasiko a6o asekwe phezu kwezi6akala, 6aza ke 
6acuntsula na6o kano6omi kuloo masiko, nakuloo mithetho 
yesiXhosa.
Kweli 6alana ndizama uku6onisaimigudu,nenkxamleko, 
nexeja elithatyathwayo ngamaXhosa xa alanda umthetho, 
ku6a kaloku kuzanyelwa uku6a uzekelwe kwisi6akala 
esakhe saakho. Ndizama noku6onisa uku6a inkosi asingu- 
yena mgqi6i wezinto yedwa, nje ngoko izizwe zi6a zona 
kunjalo kuthi.
Intetho nemikhwa yesiXhosa iya itjhona ngokutjhona 
ngenxa yeliZwi nokhanyo olukhoyo, oluze nezizwe zase- 
Ntfona-langa, oonyana 6akaGogi noMagogi.
Yindawo yomlisela nomthinjana wasemaXhoseni, uku6a 
ukhangele ngokucokisekileyo uku6a iya kuthi, yakutfhonela 
iphele le ntetho nale mikhwa inesidima yakowawo, kutjho- 
nele nto ni na emveni koko.
La ke ngoko ngamazwembe-zwembe okuzama ukuxha- 
thalaza kuloo msinga uza kutjhayela isizwe siphela. Zama- 
ni ukuwaxhasa nani ngelenu icala.
Ndingowenu emigudwini yesizwe,
EmPongo,
KweyomSintsi, 1914.
S. E. Kfune Mqhayi.

INTSAYELELO KUEICILELO LWESIEINI  
NOLWESIBOZO.
Tafuni, mzi wenkosi ndiya tafuzisa ! Namhla nje le 
ncwadana ndiya phinda ukuyi6eka phambi kwenu. Ndi- 
yi6eka namhla se inkudlwana kunokuvela kwayo, enathi 
ke nina nathetha ngezenzo uku6a ma i6uye ificilelwe 
yandiswe nokwandiswa.
Ndiya wa6ulela amaphakathi athe a6onisa iziphene kwe- 
yokuqala, anga nanamhla nje anga6uya aqokele.
Ndiya wa6ulela amanenekazi namanene ancedisileyo 
ukuyisasaza eluntwini eyokuqala ; kunga ke kunga6a ka- 
6ini kathathu ukuze nam ndinga6i sa6a madol’ anzima.
Incwadi le ithe kolu ficilelo lwesi6ini yatha6atha ngoku 
isimo se6ali lasemaXhoseni, eku6eni kolokuqala uficilelo 
yayingumzekeliso wesiXhosa.
Iingxelo ezithile ezikwiziqendu zokugqi6ela, ezimalunga 
nomEuso waPhefeya nalo unganeno, ndi6ulela ngazo 
incwadi kajustus ebale “ Izoniwo zamaXhosa.”
Kolu namhla uficilelo, sithi incwadana le se indala ma 
izithethelele ngokwayo, kumakowayo, izenzele izihlo6o 
ezizweni,—isenzele nathi indawo e6antwini 6akowethu 
esi6akhonzayo.
S. E. KrUNE MQHAYI.
ENta6’ozuko,
Berlin, C.P., 1931.

INKUNDLA.
Eli tyala lalithethelwa eGcuwa, kule ndawo inendlu  
yenkonzo ya6aNtsundu 6aseWesile ngoku, apho yayikhona  
iNkundla yaKomkhulu.
UKUMKANI.
UKumkani owayelithetha yayinguHintsa:
Umbeka-ntfiyini 6ath’ uqumbile,
Inkunz’ a6ayikhuz’ ukuhla6’ ingekahlabi.
UHintsa lowo ngunyana kaKhawuta; uKhawuta uzalwa  
nguGcaleka, uGcaleka uzalwa nguPhalo, a6e ke uPhalo  
eyinto kaTfhiwo, kaNgconde, kaTogu, kaSikhomo, ka-  
Ngcwangu, kaTfhawe, kaNkosiyamntu, kaMalangana,
kaXhosa.

“ Thetha.”
Ngeli xeja ke uDlisa noKosani 6aye kutjhonela, ku6a 
6a6engamisanga kakade.

“ KwesikaLucangwana.”
“ 'Uthe ni uLucangwana wakuyisa kuye le ndawo ? ”
“ Esi silimela sesssithathu, nkosi, ndiyisa le ndawo 
kuLucangwana.”
“ Athi ni uLucangwana ? ”
“ Ndingasuka, nkosi, ndithi, uthi uLucangwana ndidlala 
ngokuthetha, ku6a akukho sinci sakha sajola izinto zomzi, 
ikho inkulu.”
“ Inkulu ke yiyiphi ? ”
“ NguEa6ini.”
“ U tjh’ uLucangwana ? ”
“ Utfh’ uLucangwana.”
Kuthe thu kwesi sithu6a uQavile, isitye6i sasemaMvula- 
neni, negqiza lamadoda, 6esiza komkhulu apha. Eatjho 
kunene ngemi6uzo apho kundimangele, akukhov’ 
ukuqhu6a isimangalo sakhe. Bathe 6akufika kwisigqi6o 
sikaLucangwana, 6anqumama.
Ithe yaku6a le ndawo ityetyejwe yeenjiwa nje enKosini, 
ikunye namajumi oma6ini evayo amaphakathi, ityetyejwa 
nguFuzile noGqomo, igqala lasemaBambeni, 6athe 
6akutJhonela, wavakala uWisizwi, umTJhonyane, iciko 
elikhulu lakwaKhawuta, lisithi, “ Ndaza ndakuva, zwi 
ndini! ” Watjho eqongqotha inqawa. Uthe uMancapha, 
umQocwa, inkonde yakhona, “ Ndalihlala, ndalihlala eli 
phakade, ndada ndeva neenyongo zalo.” Watjho efola 
ivithi a6elifake enxhoweni ngezolo.
Uthe uMkpveqana, iqhajana elikholisa uku6a kho apha 
komkhulu, lithunywe nokuthunywa, “ Ke kaloku ixeja 
lelethu; siza kuzilungisa izint’ ezi.” Utjho ekhanda

undyilo aBelulungisela umdudo oza ku6a kwesikaSiko

ISAHLUKO II.
UKUTHETHWA KWETYALA.
Kuthe emva komdudo wakwaSiko, inKosi uHintsa 
yesusa uQavile noMdunywa, umThipha, amadoda afikisa- 
yo nathembisayo, uku6a ma 6akhe 6aye kwaLucangwana, 
6am6ize uku6a akhe eze. InKosi i6asuse se kuhlwile, 
uku6a 6aze se 6elala apho 6a6uye kusile, ukuze 6alizuze 
nethu6a lokucweya izinto ezimalunga neli tyala.
Kusoloko yathi yaliva eli tyala, inKosi ayizanga i6onakale 
yonwa6ile; yaye ke kakade i6ingenkosi ina6udlelane 
namaphakathi ngento engathi ilityala.
Uthe uQavile noMdunywa, xa 6acanda kwesika- 
Lucangwana, 6engekathi thu kowakwasi6onda umzi, 
6egqitha kumzi kaNqwakuza, umNyele, kukho iqela 
lamadoda awayeqofelisa amanqina enkatyana yenkomo 
eyayiqethulwe sisifo somgqeku. Eajikile 6a6ulisa, laduma 
iqela elo; 6athe guqaqa ngamadolo njeya, 6a6iza ilahle 
enkwenkweni. Li6aqhule kakhulu iqela elo ngokufika 
emva kokutfhata kweLawokazi, lisithi iimfene za6o se 
zindala, se zimana ukufika emva kwezithonga.
Lo gama 6aqhumisa iinqawa, 6a6uziwe imvela-phi, 
nalapho 6asinga khona; Baxela. Ea6uzwe uku6a Bona 
6avela ngakomkhulu kuxa lithi ni na ityala lento kaVuyisile 
enci. Ealandula noku6a 6akha 6eva ityala elinjalo. EaBu- 
zile ukuBa lityala lanto ni na ? Kuphendule uNqwakuza

esithi, “ A6afana ngoku njc 6athi 6akuhlutha 6asuke  
6athande ukuhamba ezinkundlcni ngohaya. Kukho mfana  
apha, iminyaka mithathu esiphcthelcezandleni, esithi ufuna 
u6ukhulu, phofu uzelwe esisinci. Loo nto siva se kusi-  
thiwa se iye nakomkhulu; 6e si6a singayira ngam, nina  
6arela ngakwelo cala.”
Bavuthulula iingubo za6o a6athunywa Begqitha, 6aya  
kugaleleka kwasiBonda ngonchwalazi. Alungiselelwa  
kakuhle la madoda kwasiBonda apha, kuBa ayesaziwa apho 
arela khona. Zaku6a ziBuziwe iindaBa, encwina umfana,  
6aye 6ee tyaa. Ancokole la madoda emveni koku, wada  
uLucangwana wa6avelisela nale nto ikhoyo yeli tyala,  
akfokfela ukuthi angaBa ufunwa ngalo apho komkhulu.  
Uwacukujele yonke into la madoda, e6uya eBuza kuwo,  
uku6a into enje ngale akhe ayiva na khona eBalini.
Kuthe ngengomso kwakukhovwa ukusengwa emini  
yakusasa, anduluka la madoda akomkhulu ukugoduka.  
Uthe kuwo uLucangwana ma ze athi uyeza; angafika  
mhlawumbi ngolwemivundla. Kwalile okunene ngo­
nchwalazi, wagaleleka uLucangwana, ehamba noMadume  
wasemaHege6eni, elinye igqala, noSigadi, indodana  
yokuhamba iBaBethela izinja. Ithe inKosi ma Bandlalelwe  
ebotwe. Yaza nayo ya6ukhathula apho ebotwe uBusuku,  
ikunye noNqhokoma umMpemvu noMalinga umNgwevu,  
amadoda a6esaziwa ngokugcina iilwimi zawo kulo lonke  
i komkhulu.
Kuthe ngoms’ o6omvu 6anduluka aBafo 6aseTho6ofane  
ukugoduka, kunga6anga kho 6ani wazileyo ukuBa 6e 6efu-  
nelwa ndawo ni na kanye-kanye yinKosi. Kuthe kaloku  
andululwa amadoda uku6a ahambe esizweni, ahambe  
exela ukuBa ngosuku lwesithathu yimbizo komkhulu.
Kwalile okunene ngomhla lowo, avela kwiinkalwana  
zonke amaphakathi, eqalele ekugqi6eleni kokusa, lada lee  
tyi ilanga ukufiya iinta6a. Zithe ziya phuma iinkomo

kwa6a kuuphela, waye umzi usithi ma kahambe, kulungile. 
Kwesi sithu6a ku6e kho umdudo kwa sesizweni apho. 
Kuthe kwaku6a kuxheliwe namhla, umlenze ongaphezulu

yawo iluvalo.

nto ni na kwaMajeke. Nguyena sel’ enguHintsa nangoku,

gwiqi uku6a 6agoduke, Beenje njalo na6amangalelwa.

aBanye 6e6onakele 6etfhayelela; a6onakele amadoda 
iyileyo iphuthuma elayo iqegu, ithi engenalo iphuthume 
umnqayi wayo ukuBa icinge ngokugoduka. Zi6onakele 
iintfayi ziqhwitha zitjhaya ; zatjho ngesisi, a6anye 6emi, 
a6anye 6esachophile, inxenye ithe guqaqa ngamadolo 
ilunyekelwa nga6anye ezinqaweni; kufumane kwathi 
xhonkxojolo. Kwaye malunga nesi sigwe6o inxenye ihu- 
mzela idela, igxeka, i6ona iindawo eziphosisiweyo ; lwaye 
uninzi lungalili6ali ityala elithetheke kakuhle, avelelwa 
onke amacala alo, sakhutjhwa ngo6uchule nesigwe6o.

uDumisani, unyana kaZolile, wasemaMpehleni, imbongi 
yakomkhulu, esithi,

nguye, yena Ea6ini. ABanye a6ooni, ngokuqonda kuka- 
Ea6ini, ngooyisekazi aa6a—uPhekesa lo kanye, ngokuthi 
oku emazi uku6a ulihilihili, kanti akazanga a6e nalizwi 
lakumthethisa, nokumBonisa izonakalo zezinto zakowaBo. 
Kwakhona, uEa6ini e6ehlala efuna ngaphakathi kwakhe 
uku6a okuya wayemgxothile lo yisekazi uPhekesa, waye- 
funa ukulungisa umzi, kwathi ni na le nto loo nto 
yaphelela emoyeni, engazanga a6uye ayithethe ? UkuBa 
u6esoyiswa kukungeva kwakhe, yena Ea6ini, phofu yinto

ni na le nto angazanga enze nomgudu omnye wokuhlanga-

kwakungacingwanga ngayo mhla mnene, ku6a wayekho.

ISAHLUKO XII.
UBUNYE BUXAKE NOMTHETHO.
Emva kweminyaka emi6ini isigwe6o siwile, kufike ilizwi 
elivela komkhulu, lisiza kuWele, lixela uku6a inKosi ifuna 
ukuza kukhuza umzi. Lithe lakufika elo lizwi kuWele, 
wamangaliswa kakhulu kuku6a lithunyelwe kuye ilizwi 
elinje, ekho umkhuluwa wakhe.
Ukhawulezile ke uWele waxelela umkhuluwa wakhe eli 
lizwi, watjho esithi yimpazamo yakomkhulu uku6a le 
ndawo isingiswe kuye ; akazani yena nayo. Le ntetho ke 
uyenze kwa ngoko, 6esekho a6athunywa aa6o 6akomkhulu.
UBUNYE BUXAKE NOMTHETHO 43
Uphendule uEa6ini wathi, ikomkhulu aliphazamanga, 
ku6a lilandela isigqi6o senkundla. Uphikile uWele wathi, 
nakanye, inkundla ayizanga igqi6e ngaloo ndlela. Kungene 
ngoku a6athunywa aa6a 6akomkhulu, 6afuna ukuqonda 
kuWele uku6a uthi yena yiyiphi na eyona ndlela kwagqi- 
tywa ngayo. Uthe yena, “ Ndithi mna eli lizwi nge 
lithunyelwa kumkhuluwa warn lo.” Eazamene 6azamana 
aa6a 6afo ngale ndawo, kwada kwa6onakala uku6a ma 
6ayifiye nje ngoko injalo.
Uthe ngoku uWele wafuna ukuqonda ku6athunywa 
apha, uku6a usuku lokuza yayilwalathile na inKosi. Bathe 
a6athunywa, inKosi ayilwalathanga usuku, ku6a ibisafuna 
ukuvakalisa indawo le kuuphela. Uthe uWele, yenzeke 
kakuhle ke loo ndawo.
Ilizwi alinikele a6athunywa lithe, “ Nothi kumHlekazi 
apho, ndiya wu6ulela lo mthetho ; kodwa phambi koku6a 
inkonzo leyo iqhu6eke, linga ilizwi lakomkhulu lingeza 
ngendlela ecacileyo.”
Eeenje njeya ke a6athunywa, 6ayinikela impendulo 
kaWele nje ngoko 6ayiphathisiweyo.
Ithe yakuviwa le mpendulo komkhulu, kwaakho ukuju- 
kuma okuthile. InKosi i6ize amaphakathi asondeleyo, 
uku6a ayivise le nto. Eve eva amaphakathi, asuke atfho 
ukwahlukana ku6ini. Amanye athi, unyanisile uWele ; 
ukhuzo alunakho ukusingiswa kuye, engenkulu. Athe 
amanye, yinkulu epheleleyo uWele, ngokomthetho owawu- 
thethiwe apha kule nkundla yakomkhulu.
I6e yenye ingxoxo enkulu ngoku leyo, ede yasuka 
yagqi6a umzi. UEa6ini ngelakhe icala uthe, nakanye 
uku6a umthetho usingiswe kuye, engenkulu, ku6a yena 
kwathethwa wagwetywa emini. Kunjalo nje wagwetywa 
ngezi6akala ezi6onwa nayimveku. Kude kwa6onakala 
uku6a umHlekazi le nto ma kade ayi6izele imbizo ethe 
nkqi, a6e kho amaphakathi awayethetha ityala.

ISAHLUKO XIV.
UKUBUBA KUKA-KHULILE, NEMBALI  
KA-NOMPUMZA.
Ithe iya fika inKosi ekhaya ukuvela kukhuza, ifika 
kukho iinto zontathu, ekuthiwe ngamadoda avela eNqa 6afa, 
aze ku 6ika uku 6a indoda enkulu, uKhulile, ayisekho.
Ke kaloku phambi koku 6a singene kwingcombolo 
yomyolelo kaKhulile, kwa kunye nemi 6ono yakhe enexa- 
6iso esizweni, siya kukhe sitsi 6e 6unkawu, senze i 6alana 
ngoNompumza iZotfho.
Kukhe kwathi, kwa phambi kwetyala eli lamawele, 
kwa6onwa kufika ndoda ithile yasezizweni, i 6onakala 
uku 6a yindoda enegama ezweni lakowayo ; yaye ihamba 
negqiza lamadoda aphantsi kwayo. Kuthe kwaku 6uzwa 
imvela-phi, yathi ivela emZimkhulu, ihamba ifuna iinya- 
makazi; ithe isizwe sayo liZotJho, igama layo nguNompu- 
mza.
Uthe kanti uNompumza lo akanyanisile ukuthi uhamba 
ezingela iinyamakazi. Eyona nyaniso yakhe yena, yingwa- 
qele yetutu, elinamaphakathi alo, laye liphethe isizwe 
sonke samatutu.
Ke kaloku ukuza kuwa apha oku, ususwa yinkosi yakhe 
kwelo zwe laseNtla. Kufike uda 6a kuyo loku 6a kukho 
“ uvuko lwa 6afileyo ” kwizwe laseXhoseni. Lwafika olu 
da 6a alwa 6a luncinane, lwaya lusanezwa ngokwanezwa.

DKumkani uHintsa yena ujike wagoduka akukhova 
ukumohlwaya uNgqika, engazi uku6a uNgqika kanti 
uye kumhalela izizwe.
Okunene ke u6uye uNgqika eThambo ngaseKho6o- 
nqa6a, apho athethene khona nezizwe eziMhlophe, 
wa6uya sel’ eyindlo6ongela. Wafika wacumza uNdlambe 
yedwa, uKumkani sel’ emkile.
Ithe xa ifikayo inKosi nama6utho ayo ekhaya, xa kuwa- 
sazelayo, xa kuthe xhonkxojolo kuzizawukawu, wavakala 
uDumisani, unyana kaZolile, wasemaMpehleni, imbongi 
yakomkhulu, wathi :

yinkcithakalo, lithe ne6ala laBo alaphumelela, nenvama 
idlekile.
Ide yeza kuBikwa le nto Komkhulu, yolu hlanga lukhoyo. 
Inkosi ifune ukuqonda uku6a ngamani na, evela phi na ? 
Uthe umzi aaBa 6antu a6azicacisi 6uni 6a6o, kuuphela into 
a6aphendula ngavo veyoku6a 6aya mfenguza.

nwe, nga6antu Bakokwayo, Baphathwe kakuhle kungaBi 
kaku6i, Bahlaliswe kwiindawo aBaya kuthi 6a6e 6odwa, 
BonwaBe kuzo, 6enze iinto zaBo, Batve Bahluthe, Baqonde 
ukuBa Bafike ekhava, kumawaBo.

yakhe .kuBa kwatfhiwo uku6izwa kolo hlanga, ngenxa 
yokuphikela kwalo ukuthi luya mfenguza) kunye nosapho 
lwayo uku6a 6aye kwalusa elo thole. Athe amanye ase6e-

lwe ngulaa mfundisi wayeze kufumayela iliZwi—uElefu, 
ekunye nenkosana eyayiphethe umkhosi,—uSomaseti.

zonke ezazinawo ! La angamaMfengu akakhutfhelwanga

uMaqoma, kwa futhi efikelwa yimikhosi ngo6usuku esi- 
zweni sakhe, kutfhiswe zindlu, kuthinjwe zinkomo, kufu- 
mane kuBe sisaqunge ; ngegama kuthiwe kufunwa iinkomo 
ezilahlekileyo. Kude kwathi kuuphi—
Wagxothwa eNchwenxa. —Isizathu sokugxothwa kwa- 
le nkosi ezweni lakowayo kuthiwe naantsi yenze isaqunge 
sokulwa, kwatjhiwo ngento awasuka uMaqoma waya 
kunceda uEawana inkosi yomThembu eyayivukelwe 
ngumphakathi wayo, wayigeBenga, okunene ke uMaqoma 
ku6a wayehlatyelwe, wamchitha umphakathi lowo, aka- 
thimba nento ngaphandle kokuqhu6a amakfoma okutya 
kwamadodana. Ithe ke loo nto yaasisizathu sokugxothwa 
kwakhe kusithiwa ngumntu kaFulumente Iowa amchithi- 
leyo. Inkosana yamajoni eyaviphethele loo mse6enzi wo- 
kufaka uMaqoma uviko nguColonel Somerset, unyana 
kaLord C. Somerset. Yeenza imise6enzi engendawo 
inkosana leyo kakhulu, Bada Bathimla aBafundisi iinto 
zooRoss umfo owakha wabalelana incwadi ezifaBaxa 
noFulumente ngayo loo nto. UDr. Philip indoda eBi- 
mxhalise kunene uFulumente, yakha yeenza ukuBakuBekho 
ingxoxo enkulu ngayo le mpatho, isithi : “ Aa6a 6antu
nga6akarulumente 6o6a6ini, ezi nkosi zaBaThembu, ke 
uku6a uFulumente u6efuna ukwenza uBulungisa, ngel’  
eyitha6athele kuye le ngxa6ano isencinane, wohlwaya lo 
uBulele omnye, yaBa loo nto iphele engekade ahlatyelwe 
uMaqoma.”

Amatyala.—Izizwe ezimaziyo uMaqoma, ezimhlophe 
nezintsundu, zivumelene uku6a u6enganele kuBa likhalipha

ko engathi uthe ehleli wa6e sel’ ewacwangcisile, nje ngoko 
se sikhe saziva impendulo zakhe kwiruluneli ezikhe

mpendulo zayo. Oku konke kwenzelwa ukuze athi ogwe- 
tywayo agwetywe ngamazwi akhe, okanve ngamazwi 
aamanqhina akhe.

kunika uBunqhina Balo enkundleni apho ; koko indoda

nza oko ; kakade yafunvanwa ingathethanga nto iseke- 
kileyo.

“ Ilizwe liya fa.” Akhawuleza a 6iza iruluneli uSmiti,

owayeyimantyi enkulu yamaXhosa. Ijike yemka ingabo-

“ USandile woyika wena yise wakhe, akezanga.”

andovikayo ? ”

ITuluneli (juju): “ Hayi, mbizeni uSandile abe kho.”

ITuluneli (ngomsindo): “ HlaT uthi tu, nxila ndini,
uthetha nabani wena ! ”
Maqoma : “ E : Wanga nguwen’ unxilileyo ! ”
Ithe ke iTuluneli levo ebufufwini yahlokomisa esizweni

a“ Bantu.” Ngoko ke kukuthi ukuyiphelisa into esavivuma 
singayazi, samkele into esayichasa kwa ngokungaqondi.
Iimfazwe. —Phambi kokuBa siBuyele kwindawo yethu 
yenkqu 6ela, kuhle ukuBa sikhe sivixele ukuBa ayihamba- 
nga kakuhle, ngenxa yofofozi lweemfazwe esizilwe nama- 
gwangqa, zasisixakaniso ke phambi kwenkquBela phambili. 
Kwincwadi ezayo sichukuje kakhulu izizathu zeemfazwe 
kwa neemfazwe eziBe khoyo kumaXhosa, kuBeSuthu, 
kwaZulu, kumaNdeBele, nakwiAfrika eseZantsi iphela. 
Ngoku ke apha azikuBa nandawo.
Ezi mfazwe ziluthotho, kuthiwa xa kuthethwayo ziqalwa 
sithi Bantsundu : (1) ngokugqitha imida ekuvunyelweneyo 
ngayo. (2) Ngo 6usela Beenkomo zamagwangqa.
